The following actions are running on Node.js 20 and may not work as expected: actions/checkout@v4, actions/setup-java@v4, actions/setup-node@v4, android-actions/setup-android@v3, pnpm/action-setup@v4. To opt into Node.js 24 now, set the FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24=true environment variable on the runner or in your workflow file. Once Node.js 24 becomes the default, you can temporarily opt out by setting ACTIONS_ALLOW_USE_UNSECURE_NODE_VERSION=true.
